MINISTRY OF TAW, JUSTICE & COMPANY AFFAms 
(Department of Legal Affairs) 

New Delhi, the 25th July, 1981 

G,S.R. 745. — In exercise of the powers conferred by 
clause (a) of rule 8B of Order XXVII of the First Schedule 
to lh e Code of Civil Procedure 1908 (5 of 1908), the Central 
Government hereby makes the following further amendment 
in the notification of the Government of India in the late 
Ministry of Law No. G.S.R. 1412 dated the 25th November, 
1960, namely :— 

In the Schedule to the said notification, in item 5 relating 
to Kerala, against sub-item (a) relating to High Court, in 
column (2), (it for the existing entries, the following en- 
tries shall be substituted, namely : — 

“O') Shri M. M. Abdul Aziz, Senior Central Govern- 
ment Standing Counsel. 

01) Shi i P. C. Chacko, Additional Central Government 
Standing Counsel.’’ 

[No. F. 36(5) /8 1-Judl.] 
K. C. D. GANGWANI, Additional Legal Adviser

MINISTRY OF HOME AFFAIRS 

New Delhi, the 1st August, 1981 

G.S.R. 746. — In exercise of the powers conferred by the 
proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President hereby 
makes the following rules regulating the method of rec- 
ruitment to the post of Research Officer namely :— 

1. Short title and commencement. — (1) These rules may 
be called the Sardar Vallabhbhai Patel National Police Aca- 
demy Research Officer, Recruitment Rules, 1981.. 

(21 They shall come into force on the date of their 
publication in the Official Gazette. 

2. Number of posts, classification and scale of pay. — The 
number of the said post, classification and scale of pay 
attached thereto shall be as specified in columns 2 to 4 of 
the Schedule annexed hereto. 

3. Method of recruitment, age limit and qualifications 
etc. — The method of recruitment, age limit, qualifications 
and other matters relating to the said post shall be as speci- 
fied In columns 5 to 13 of the Schedule aforesaid, 

4. Disqualification.— No person — • 

(a) who has entered into or contracted a marriage with 
a, person having, a spouse living, or 


(b) who, having a spouse living, has entered into or 
contracted a marriage with any person, 
shall be eligible for appointment to the said post : 

Provided, that the Central Government may, if satisfied 
that such marriage 1 $. ptrmisdble under the personal law 
applicable to such -p&son and the other party to the marriage 
and there arc other grounds for so doing, exempt any person 
from the operation of this rule. 

5. Power to relax, — Wheic the Central Government is of 

opinion that it is necessary or expedient so to 'J, it may, 
by order, for reasons to be lccoided in wriLiq# and in con- 
sultation with the Union Public Service ission relax 

any of the piovlsions of these mles witWrespcct to any 
class or category of persons. 

6. Saving.— Nothing in these rules shall affect reservations, 
relaxation of age limit and other concessions required to be 
provided for the Schedule Castes, the Scheduled Tribes and 
other special categories of persons in accordance with the 
oiders issued by the Central Government from time to time 
in this regard.

(Department of Personnel and Administrative Reforms) 

New Delhi, the 15th July, 1981 

GiJ.R. 747. — In exercise of the powers conferred by the 
roviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President 
ereby makes the following rules further to amend the 
Indian Economic Service Rules, 1961, namely : — 

1. (1) These Rules may be called the Indian Economic 

Service (Second Amendment) Rules, 1981, 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their 
publication in the Official Gazette. 

2. In the Indian Economic Service Rules. 1961, in sub- 
rule (i) of rule 8, in clause (a), — - 

(1) for sub-clause (i), the following shall b a substituted 
namely : — 

“60 per cent of the vacancies in this grade shall be 
filled by direct recruitment through an open com- 
petitive examination to be held by the Commission 
in the manner prescribed in Schedule IT*. 

(2) In sub-clause (ii), for th e words “Not more than 25 
per cent", the words "40 per cent” shall be substituted,

G.S.R, 748. — In exercise of the powers conferred by the 
proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President 
hereby makes the following i tiles further to amend the 
Indian Statistical Service Rules, 1961, namely 

1. (1) These Rules may be called the Indian Statistical 

Service (Fourth Amendment) Rules, 1981. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their 
publication in the Official Gazette. 

2. In the Indian Statistical Service Rules, 1961, in sub- 
rule (i) of rule 8, in clause (a) : — 

(1) for sub-clause (i), th e following shall be substituted 
namely : — 

“60 per cent of the vacancies in this grade shall be 
filled by direct recruitment through an open com- 
petitive examination to be held by the Commission 
in the manner prescribed in Schedule TI”. 

(2) in sub-clause (li), for the words “Not more than 25 
per cent” the words “40 per cent” shall be substituted. 

[F. No. 11025/10/80-ES] 
K. V. SAMPATH KUMAR, Under Secy.

New Delhi, the 7th August, 1981 
MEDICINAL AND TOILET PREPARATIONS 

G.S.R. 752. — In pursuance of clause (h) of section 2 of 
the Medicinal and Toilet Preparation (Excise Duties) Act. 
1955 (16 of 1955) the Central Government hereby declares 
the following substances to be narcotic drugs, for the purposes 
of the said Act namely: —

New Delhi, the 15th August, 1981 

G5.R. 754. — 'In exercise of the powers conferred by sec- 
tion 37 of the Central Excises and Salt Act, 1944 (1 of 1944). 
the Central Government hereby makes the following rules 
further to amend the Central Excise Rules, 1944. namely :— 

1. (1) These rules may be called the Central Excise 

(19th Amendment) Rules, 1981. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their 
publication in the Official Gazette. 

2. In rule 185 of the Central Excise Rules. 1944 (herein- 
after referred to as the said rules), — 

(a) in sub-rule (1) — 

(i) in clause (a), for the words “by sea through the 
ports” the words “by air or sea through the air- 
ports or ports, as the case may be,” shall be subs- 
tituted ; 


(ii) iu clause (b), for the words “by sea through other 
ports” the words “by air or sea through other aii- 
ports Or ports, as the case rp a y be.” sh ill be substi- 
tuted ; 

(b) in sub-rule (4A), for the words “Exports by sea, 
through the ports” the words "Exports by au or 
sea, through the airports or ports, as the case may 
be" shall Ire substituted ; 

(c) in sub-rule (4B), for the words “by sea through any 

port other than the ports" the words "by air or 
sea through any airport or port, as the case may 
be, other than the airports or ports, as the case 
may be,” shall be substituted. 

3. In rule 187 of the said rules, in sub-rule (1), — 

(a) for the words “exports by sea” the words "exports 

by alf or sea, a s the case may be,” shall be substi- 
tuted ; 

(b) iu clause (A), — 

(i) for the words. “by sea through the poits” the \xords 
“by air or sea through the airports or ports, as 
the case may be,” shall be substituted ; 

(ii) for the words "port of export” the words "air port 
or port of export, as the case may be,” shall 
be substituted. 

4 In rule 189 A of the said rules, — 

(a) in the heading, foi the words “by sea through the 
ports” the words “by air or sea through the air- 
ports or ports, as the case may be,” shall be 
substituted ; 

lb) for the words “at the port of export” the words “at 
the airport or port of export, as the case may be,” 
shall be substituted, 

5. In Appendix I to the said rules, under the heading “(II) 
Specimen Forms", — 

(a) in Central Excise Series No. 18, in the second para- 

graph, for the brackets and words “(port or land 
customs station of export)", the brackets and words 
“(airport, port or land customs station of export)" 
shall be substituted ; 

(b) in Centinl Excise Series No. 60 — 

(i) in the heading, for the words “by Sea /Port" 

the words “by Air /Sea /Post" shall be substi- 
tuted ; 

(ii) before the Table, for the words “by sea/parcel 

post” the woids “by air/sea/parcel post" shall 
be substituted ; 

(iii) aftet paragraph 1 below the Table, for the words 

“Exports by Sea” the words "Fxporl by Air/ 
Sea” shall be substituted ; 

llv) in paragraph 4 below the Table, for the words 
"any port” the words “any airport or port” shall 
he substituted. 

(c) in Central Excise Series No. 60-A, — 

Ci) in the heading, for the words, 'by Sea /Post/ 
hand” the words “by Air/Sea/Post/Land” shall 
be substituted ; 

(ii) before the Table, for the words “by Sea/Land” 

the words "by Air/Sea/Land" shall be substitu- 
ted ; 

(iii) in paragraph 3 below the Table for the brackets 

and words “(For exports by Sea)’’ the brackets 
and words “(For exports by air or sea, ns the 
ease may be)" shaft be substituted. 

(Notification No. 155/81-CE/F, No, 209/10/79-CX 61 

D. MEHTA, Under Secy.

New Delhi, the 28th July, 1981 

G.S.R. 756. — Whereas a draft of certain rulc6 fuither to 
amend the Petroleum Rules, 1976, was published as requited 
by sub-section (2) of section 29 of the Petroleum Act, 1934 
(30 of 1934), with the notification of the Government of 
India in the Ministry of Industiy (Department of Industrial 
Development) No. G.S.R. 32, dated the 24th December, 1980 
at page 55 of the Gazette of India, Part II— Section 3 — Sub- 
section (i), dated the 10th January, 1981 inviting objections- 
or suggestions from all persons likely to be alfected thereby 
before the expiry of 45 days from the date on which the 
copies of the Official Gazette in which flic notification was 
published were made available to the public. 

And whereas the copies of the said Official Gazette were 
made available to the public on the 17th January, 1981. 

And whereas no objections or suggestions were received 
from the public on the said draft by the Cential Government; 

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by 
section 4, sub-section (2) of section 5, sub section ;2) of 
section 14, sections 21 and 22 and sub-section (1) of section 
29 of die Petroleum Act, 1934 (30 of 1934), the Central 
Government heieby makes the following, tales further to 
amend the Petroleum Rules, 1976, namely : — 

1, (Ij These rules may be called the Petroleum (2nd 
Amendment) Rules, 1981. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their 
publication in the Official Gazette. 

t 2. In lule 168 of the Petroleum Rules, 1976, for the words 
“nearer than 30 metres”, the words “nearer than 90 metres 
lo any still boiler or furnace or nearer than 30 metres” shall 
be substituted.

New Delhi, the 29th July, 1981 

G.S.R. 757.— Whereas a draft of ceituin mles further to 
amend the Petroleum Rules, 1976, was published as required 
by sub-section (2) of section 29 of the Petroleum Act, 1934 
(30 of 1934), with the notification of the Government of 
India in the Ministry of Industry (Department of Industrial 
Development) No, G.S.R. 252 dated the 23rd February, 1981, 
at page 503 ol the Gazette of India, Part If Section 3 Sub- 
section (i), dated the 7th March, 1981, inviting objections 
or suggestions from all pereons likely to be affected (hereby 
before the expiry of 45 days from the date on which the 
copies of the Official Gazette in which the notification was 
published wete made available to the public. 

And whereas the copies of the said Official Gazette wet® 
made available to the public on the 1st April, 1981. 

And whereas no objections or suggestions were received 
from the public on the said draft by the Central Govern- 
ment ; 

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by 
section 4, sub-section (2) of the section 5, sub-section (2) 
of section 14, sections 21 and 22 and sub section (1) of 

section 29 of the Petroleum Act, 1934 (30 of 1934), the 

Central Government hereby makes the foliowing rules 
further to amend the Petroleum Rules, namely : — 

1. (1) These rules may be called the Petroleum (3rd 

Amendment) Rules, 1981, 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their 
publication in the Official Gazette. 

2. Rule 146 of the Petroleum Rules, 1976, shall be 

omitted.

1864 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : AUGUST 15, 1981/SRAVANA 24, 1903 [Part II— Sec. 3(i) 


amend the All-India Institute of Medical Sciences Rules, 1958, 
namely ; — 

1. (1) These rules may be called the All-India Institute of 
Medical Sciences (Amendment) Rules. 1981. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their pub- 
lication in the Official Gazette, 

2. In the All India Institute of Medical Sciences Rules, 1958 
(hereafter referred to as the said rules) for rule 3. the fol .ow- 
ing rule shall be substituted, namely ; — 

“(3) Nomination of representatives of Medical Faculties-^ 
for the purpose of nomination of members under 
clause (f) of section 4 of the Act, the Vice-Chancellor 
of every Indian University having a medical facutly 
may recommend one person from the medical faculty 
of the University, From among the persons so re- 
commended the Central Government shall nominate 
four persons to be members of the Institute having 
due regard to different disciplines." 

3. In rule 7 of the said rules, — 

(1) for sub-rules (1) and (2) the following sub-rules shall be 

substituted, namely : — 

"(1) The Institute may create posts, subject to specific 
provision in the budget, on scales of pay applicable to 
similar posts under the Government or on scales of 
pay approved by the Government, classify them into 
grades and specify their designations : 

Provided that no post above the Associate Professor’s 
level shall be created except with prior approval of 
the Government. 

(2) The Director General of Health Services shall be a 

member of all Selection Committees constituted by 
the Institute for recruitment to faculty posts." 

(ii) after sub-rule (3), the following sub-rule shall be inser- 
ted, namely : — 

‘‘(4) In the event of the Director proceeding on leave, 
resigning or retiring, or the post falling vacant in 
any other eventuality, till such time, a new Direc- 
tor is appointed, the President may appoint, the 
senior most Professor to look after the functions of 
the Director for a period not exceeding six months: 


Provided that the Institute may appoint, for reason* to 
be recorded in writing, any other person for a 
period not exceeding six months : 

Provided further that if the period of such, appointment 
exceeds six months prior approval of the Govern- 
ment shall be taken for sudh appointment.” 

4. In rule 8, of the said rules, fbr part I and part II and 
the entries relating thereto, the following shall be substituted, 
namely 

■‘Part I relating to Non-Plra expenditure and Part 11 relat- 
ing to Plan expenditure.” 

5. In rule 9 of the said rules, — 

(i) for sub-rule (1). thr following sub-rule shall be substi- 

tuted, namely : — 

“(1) All moneys e'edited to the Fund shalt be deposited 
In any brands of the State Bank of India or th* 
nationalisedBank in New Delhi. 

(ii) for sub-rule (f), the following sub-rule shall be substi- 

tuted, namjty : — 

“(3) All bffif for payment shall be prechecked bv the 
Accoi/ts Officer of the Institute." 

6. for rule l/ of the said rules, the following shall be substi- 
tuted, namely 

"(10) A/wal Statement of Accounts: The annual sta te- 
mpt of accounts including the balance-sheet of the 
Iymtute shall be in such form as may be laid down 
f the Government. The statement pertaining to 
kch year ending with 3 1st March together with the 
audit report thereon shall be forwarded annually to 
the Government together with auch number Of spare 
copies thereof as may be required by the Government 
from time to time but not later than the 3 1st Decem- 
ber following.”

New Delhi, the 29th July, 1981 

G.S.R. 759. In exercise of the powers conferred by the 

proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President hereby 
makes the following rules restating the method of lecruitment 
of persons to the post of Stores Officer (General Stores) in the 
vSutdarjang Hospital, New Delhi, namely 

Short title and commencement,— (1) These rules may be 
called the Safdarjang Hospital, New Delhi, Stores Officer 
(General Stores') Recruitment Rules, 1981. 

(2) They shall come into foicc on the date of ffielr publi- 
cation in the Official Gazette. 

2. Number. Classification and scale of pay.— The number of 
post, 1 C classification and the scale of pay attached thereto 
shall be as specified lit columns 2 to 4 of the Schedule annexed 
to these rules. 

3. Method of recmitnNnl, age limit and othci qualifica- 
tions. — The method of recruitment, age limit, qualification 
and other matters connected therewith shall be as specified 
in columns 5 to 13 of the Schedule aforesaid. 


4. Disqualification, — No person, 

(a) who has entered into or contracted a maniage with a 

person having a spouse living, or 

(b) who having a spouse living, has entered into or con- 

tracted a mairiage with any pci son, 

shall be eligible tor appointment to the said post ; 

Provided that the Central Government may, if satisfied that 
such marriage is permissible under the personal law applicable 
to such person and the other party to the marriage and that 
there are othci grounds for so doing, exempt any person from 
the operation of this 1 ule. 

5. Power to relax. — Where the Central Government is of 
opinion that It is necessary or expedient so to do, it may, by 
order, foi reasons to be recorded in writing, relax, in consul- 
tation with the Union Public Service Commission, any of the 
provisions of Ihcse rules with respect to any class or catcgorv 
or persons. 

6. Saving. — Nothing m these rules shall affect reservations 
relaxation of age limit and othci concessions lequired to be 
provided for the Scheduled Castes, the Scheduled Tribes and 
other such categories of persons in accordance with the orders 
issued by the Central Government from time to lime in this 
regard.

New Delhi, the 28th July, 1 98 1 

G.S.R, 760. ---Whereas the Central Government is of opinion that for the conservation and development of minerals in 
India it is necessary to collect as precise information ai possible with regard to any mineral available in or under the lease hold areas 
specified in the Table below: — 

And wh’je s in respect of the said areas, mining leases have been granted by the State Government of Tamil Nadu; 

Now therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by sub-section (1) of section 18A of the Mines and Minerals (Regulation 
and Development) Act, 1957 the Central Government, after consultation with the State Government of Tamil Nadu as required by the 
proviso to sub-section (1 ) of the said section, 1 8A, hereby authorise the Geological Survey of India to carryout such detailed investiga- 
tions for the purpose of obtaining such information as may be necessary in the areas specified in the said Tahle.

New Delhi, the 29th July, 1981 

G.S.R, 761.™ In exercise of the powers conferred by the 
proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President hereby 
makes the following rules further to amend the Geological 
Smvey of India (Group A and Group B Posts) Recruitment 
Rules, 1967, namely : — 

r, (1) These rules may be called the Geological Survey of 
India (Group A and Group B Posts) Recruitment (Fouith 
Amendment) Rules, 1981. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their publi- 
cation in the Official Gazette. 

2. In the Schedule to the Geological Survey of India 
(Gump A and Group B Posts) Recruitment Rules, 1967 : — 

(a) against serial number 4 relating to the post of 

Director; — 

(i) for the entry under column 1, the following entry 

shall be substituted, namely : — 

“Director (Geology)”; 

(ii) the following Note shall be added below the entry 

under column 2, namely ; — 

“Note. — Subject to variation depending on work- 
load.”; 

(b) against serial number 10 relating to the post of 

Superintending Chemist,— 

(i) for the entry under column t, the following entry 

shall be substituted, namely ; — . 

“Director (Geo-Chemistry)”; 

(ii) the following Note shall be added below the entry 

under column 2. namely : — 

“Note. — Subject to variation depending on work- 
load.” 

(c) against serial number 1? relating to the poit of 

Superintending Me.hanical Engineer, — 

(i) for the entry under column 1 the following entry 

shall be substituted, namely : — 

“Director (.Mechanical Engineering)”; 

(ii) the following Note shall be added below the entry 

under column 2, namely : — 

“Note. — Subject to variation depending on Work- 
load.” 

(d) against serial number 24 relating to the post of 

Superintending Geophysicist, 

(i) for the entry under column 1, the following entry 

shall be substituted, namely 

"Director (Geophysics) 1 ’; 

(ii) the following Note shall be added below the 

entiy under column 2. namely; 

“Note.— Subject to variation depending on wort- 
Joad.

Note. — Geological Suivcy of India (Group A and Group B 
posts) Recruitment Rules, 1967 were published In (he Gazette 
of India Part II Section 3 Sub-section (i) on 16th Decern Pci, 
1967 as GSR 1883. These rules were subsequently amended 
vide notification published in the Gazette of India on 4th 
June, 1969 as G.S.R. 1515. Recruitment Rules for the posts 
of Senior Cod Accounts Officer and Superintending Minera- 
logist were published in the Gazette of India on 28th April, 
197.9 as GSR 598 and on 24th November, 1979 as GSR 1397 
respectively.

New Delhi, the 3rd August, 1981 

G..S.R. 764. — In exercise of the powers conferred by 
the proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President 
hereby makes the following rules, namely: — 

1. Short title and commencement.— 11 ) These rules may 
be called the Ministry of Agriculture and Irrigation (Depart- 
ment of Irrigation) (Ganga Basin Water Resources Organi- 
sation) Non-Ministerial Group ‘A’ and Group ‘B’ Posts Re- 
cruitment (Repeal) Rules, 1981. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their publi- 
cation in the Official Gazette. 

2. Repeal and Saving. — The Ministry of Agriculture and 
Irrigation (Department of Irrigation) (Ganga Basin Water 
Resources Organisation) Non-Ministerial Group ‘A’ and 
Group ‘B’ Posts Recruitment Rules, 1963, are hereby re- 
pealed, save as especls things done ot omitted to be done, 
before such repeal.

THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : AUGUST 15, 1981/SRAVANA 24, 1903 


[Part II— Sec. 3(i)] 


MINISTRY OF EDUCATION & CULTURE 
(Department of Education) 

BUREAU FOR PROMOTION OF URDU 
New Delhi, the 30th July, 1981 

GAR 765.— In exercise of the powers conferred 
the proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President 
hereby makes the following rules regulating the method of 
recruitment to the post Of Safaiwala-cum-Frash in the Bureau 
for Promotion of Urdu, namely : — 

1. Short title and commencement. — (1) These rules may 
be called the Bureau for Promotion of Urdu (Safaiwala- 
cum-Frash) Recruitment Rules, 1981. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their publi- 
cation in the Official Gazette. 

2. Number of post, classification and scale of pay.— The 
number of the said post, its classification and the scale of 
pay attached thereto shall be as specified in columns 2 to 4 
of the Schedule annexed to these rules. 

3. Method of recruitment, age limit, and other qualifica- 
tions etc. — The method of recruitment, age limit, qualifications 
and other matters relating to the said post shall be as speci- 
fied in columns 5 to 13 of the Schedule aforesaid. 


Name of post No, of Classification Scale of Pay 
Posts 


12 3 4 


‘Safaiwala-cum- 

Prash* 


‘Subject to variatin 


1* General Central Rs. 196-3-220- 
Service, Group EB-3-232. 

'D* Non-Gazet- 
tcd. 

dependent on work-load. 


4. Disqualification. — No person, 

(a) who has entered into or contracted a marriage with 

a person having a spouse living, or 

(b) who, having a spouse living, has entered into or 

contracted a marriage with any person, shall be eligi- 
ble for appointment to the said post : 

Provided that the Central Government may, if satisfied, 
that such marriage is permissible under the personal law 
applicable to such person and the other party to the mar- 
riage and that there are other grounds for so doing, exempt 
any person from the operation of this rule. 


5. Power to relax. — Where the Central Government is of 
opinion that it is necessary or expedient so to do, it may, 
by order, for reasons to be recorded in writing, relax any of 
the provisions of these rules with respect to any class or 
category of persons. 

6. Saving. — Nothing in these rules shall affect reservations, 
relaxation of age limit and other concessions required to be 
provided for the Scheduled Castes, the Scheduled Tribes and 
other special categories of . persons in accordance with the 
orders issued by the Central Government from time to time 
in this regard.

1882 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : AUGUST 15, 1981/SRAVANA 24, 1903 [Part II— Sec. 3(i)] 


MINISTRY OF SOCIAL WELFARE 

New Delhi, the 20th June, 1981 

G.S.IL 766.— In exercise of the powers conferred by the 
proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President hereby 
makes the following rules regulating the method of recruit- 
ment to tile posts of Junior Analyst (Works Study) and Re- 
search Assistant (Works Study) in the Ministry of Social 
Welfare namely : — 

1, (1) Short title and commencement,— (1) These rules 
may be called the Ministry of Social Welfare Junior Analyst 
(Works Study) and Research Assistant (Works Study) Recruit- 
ment Rules, 1981. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their publi- 
cation in the Official Gazette. 

2, Number, classification and scale of pay. — The number 
of the posts, their classification and the scales of pay attached 
thereto shall be as specified in columns 2 to 4 of the Sche- 
dule annexed thereto. 

3, Method of recruitment, age limit and other qualifica- 
tions. — The method of recruitment, age limit, qualifications 


and other matters relating to the said posts shall be as speci- 
fied in columns 5 to 13 of the said schedule. 

4. Disqualifications. — No person, 

(a) who has entered into or contracted a marriage with 

a person having a spouse living, or 

(b) who, having a spouse living, has entered Into oi 

contracted a marriage with any person, 

shall be eligible for appointment to the said post . 

Provided that the Central Government may, If satisfied 
that such marriage is permissible under the personal law 
applicable to such person and the other party to the marriage 
and there are other grounds for so doing, exempt any person 
from the operation of this rule. 

5. Power to relax. — Where the Central Government Is of 
the opinion that it is necessary or expedient to do, it may, 
by order, for reasons to be recorded m writing and in con- 
sultation with the Union Public Service Commission, relax any 
of the provisions of these rules with respect to any 
class or category of posts or persons.

MINISTRY OF TOURISM & CIVIL AVIATION 
New Delhi, the 27th July, 1981 

G.S.R. 773. — The following draft of certain rules further to amend the Aircraft Rules, 192 7 which the Central Government 
propose to make in exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Aircraft Act, 1934 (22 of 1934) is hereby published as required 
by section 14 of the said Act for the information of all persons likely to be effected thereby, and notice is hereby given that the draft 
will b; taken into consideration after a period of three months from the date of publication of this notification in the Official Gazette. 

2. Any objections or suggestions which may be received from any person with respect to the said druft rules before the period so 
spxifled will be considered by the Central Government. 


DRAFT RULES 

1. rb;se rules mty be called the Aircraft ( Amendment) Rules, 1981. 

2. In Schedule I to the Aircraft Rules, 1937, after the item No. (2), the following shall be inserted, namely : — 

Area Extent of prohibition 

“(3) The area bounded by following co-ordinates : 

2837 N 771145 E 

2837 N 771230 E 

283630 N 771230 E Absolute” 

283630 N 771145 E 

extending vertically from ground level to tin unlimited upper level.

1 HE GAZETTE OF INDIA : AUGUST 15, 1981/SRAVANA 24, 1903 [Part II— Sec. 3( i)] 


MINISTRY OF INFORMATION & BROADCASTING 
New Delhi, the 17th June, 1981 

G.S.R. 774. — In exercise of the* powers conferred by the 
proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President hereby 
makes the following rules regulating the method of recruit- 
ment to the posts of Senior Analyst (Works Study), Junior 
Analyst (Works Study) and Research Assistant (Works Study) 
in the Ministry of Information & Broadcasting namely : 

1. (1) Short title and commencement.— (1) These rules 
may be called the Ministry of Information and Broadcasting 
Senioi Analyst (Works Study), Junior Analyst (Works Study) 
and Research Assistant (Works Study) Recruitment Rules, 
1980, 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their publi- 
cation in the Official Gazette. 

2, Number, classification and scale of pay. — The number 
of the posts, their classification and th e scales of pay attach- 
ed thereto shall be as specified in columns 2 to 4 of the 
Schedule Annexed hereto. 


3. Method of recruitment, age limit and other qualifica- 
tions.— -The method of recruitment, age limit, qualifications 
and other matters relating to the said posts shall be as 
specified in columns 5 to 13 of the said schedule, 

4. Disqualifications. — No person, — 

(a) who has entered inlo or contracted a marriage with 

a person having a spouse living, or 

(b) who, having a spouse living, has entered into oi con- 

tracted a marriage with any person, 

shall be eligible for appointment to the said post : 

Provided that the Central Government may, if satisfied that 
such marriage is permissible under the personal law appli- 
cable to such person and the other party to the marriage and 
there are other grounds for so doing, exempt any person 
from the operation of this rule. 

5. Power to relax. — Where the Central Government is of 
the opinion that it is necessary or expedient so to do, it may, 
by order, for reasons to be recorded in writing and in con- 
sultation with the Union Public Service Commission, relax 
any of the provisions of these rules with respect to any 
class or category of posts or persons.

New Delhi, the 29th luly, 1981 

G.S.R. 775 — In exercise of the powers conferred by 
the proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President 
hereby makes th c following rules to amend the Central 
Board of Film Censors, Bombay (lunior Stenographer and 
Mazdoor) Recruitment Rules, 1980*, namely : — 

1. (i) These rules may b c called the Central Board of Film 
Censors, Bombay (Junior Stenographer and Mazdoor) Re- 
cruitment (Amendment) Rules, 1981. 

(ii) They shall come into force on the date of their publi- 
cation in the Official Gazette. 

2. in the Schedule to the Central Board of Film Censors, 
Bombay (Junior Stenographei and Mazdoor) Recruitment 
Rules. 1980, against serial number 1 relating to the post of 

'‘Stenographer Grade III" ; 

(i) for ihe entry under column 2 the following entry 

sh ill be substituted, namely : — 

"Stenographer Grade III”; 

(ii) after the entry under column 7 the following note 

sfraTI be inserted, namely : — 

"Note, — The crucial date for determining the age 
limit shall b e the closing date for receipt of 
applications from candidates in India (other than 
those in Andaman and Nicobar Islands and Lak- 
shadweep). In the case of recruitment made through 
the Employment Exchange the crucial date for 
determining the age limit shall be the last date 
upto which the Employment Exchange is asked 
to submit the names. 

‘Published in the Gazelle of India dated the 28th February, 
1981 under the notification of the Government of India in 
th c Ministry of Information and Broadcasting No. G.S.R. 241 
dated the 9th February, 198f. 

[File No. 802/9/80-T(C)I 
K. S, VENKATARAMAN, Desk Officer

INDIAN POSTS & TELEGRAPHS DEPARTMENT 
Office of the Director General of Posts & Telegraphs 
New Delhi, the 9th June, 1981 

G.S.R. 777. — In exercise of the powers conferred 
by section 10 of the Indian Wireless Telegraphy Act, 1933 
(17 of 1933), the Central Government hereby makes the 
following rules further to amend the Indian Wireless Tele- 
graphy (Possession) Rules, 1965, namely:— 

1 . These rules may be called the Indian Wireless Tele- 
graphy (Possession) (Amendment) Rules, 1981. 

2. In the Indian Wireless Telegraphy (Possession) Rules, 
1965, in rule 19 for clause (if), the following clauses shall 
be substituted; namely; — 

”(ii) in the case of a dealer, if he had a licence, which 
has expired on 31st December, but has not been re- 
newed °n or before the last post office working day 
in the month, surcharge at the rate of rupees two 
per calender month or part thereof, after the month 
of December aforementioned ; 

(iii) in the case of any other, if he had a licence which 
has expired on 3 1st December, but has not been 
renewed on or before the last post office working 
day in the month of January following, surcharge 
at fhe rate of rupee one per calender month 
or part thereof, after the month of January afore- 
mentioned. 

[No. 1-74/75-BRL] 
Y. D. SARDANA, Asstt. Director General (BRI.)

MINISTRY OF HEALTH AND FAMILY WELFARF 
(Department of Health) 

New Delhi, the 30th June, 1981 

G.S.R. 780. — In exercise of the powers conferred by the 
proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President heieby 
makes the folio wins rules regulating the method of reciuit- 
ment to the Post of Senior Training Officer (Non-medical) at 
the Rural Health Training Centre, Najafgarh, a Subordinate 
Office of the Ministry of Health and Family Welfare. 

1. Short title and commencement — (1) These rules may be 
called the Rural Health Training Centre, Najafgarh, Senior 
Training Officer (Non-medical) Recruitment Rules, 1981. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their 
publication in the Official Gazette. 

2. Number, classification and scale of pay. — The number Of 
the posts, its classification and the scale of pay attached there- 
to shall be as specified in columns 2 to 4 of the Schedule 
annexed to these rules. 

3. Method of recruu^^K, age limit aud other qualifica- 
tions. — The method of ^^pitment, age limit, Qualifications 
and other matters relating^® the said post shall be as speci- 
fied in columns 5 to 13 of me said schedule 


(a) who has entered into or contracted a marriage \titb 

a person having a spouse living, or 

(b) who, having a spouse living, has entered into or con- 

tracted a marriage with any person, 

shall be eligible for appointment to the said post • 

Provided that the Central Government, may if satisfied that 
such marriage is permissible under the personal law appli- 
cable to such person and the other paity to the marriage 
and tbat 'there are other grounds for so doing, exempt any 
person from the operation of this rale. 

5. Power to relax. — Where the Central Government is of 
opinion, that it is necessaty O’- expedient to do so it may, 
J>v order, for leasons to be recorded in writing and in con- 
sultation with the Union Public Service Commission relax any 
of the provisions of these lules with respect to any class or 
category or person. 

6. Saving. — Nothing in these rules shall affect reservations, 
relaxation of age limit and other concessions required to be 
provided for the Scheduled Castes, the Scheduled Tribes and 
other special categoiies of persons in accordance with the 
orders issued by the Central Government from time to time 
in this regard

MINISTRY OF AGRICULTURE 

(Department of Agriculture & Cooperation) 

New Delhi, the 5th August, 1981 

G.S.R. 781. — Tn exercise of the powers conferred by the 
proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President hereby 
makes the following rules further to amend the Forest Re- 


[qpr II— 3(i )] 


qrrq qr TrsTTsf : ?nrffT 22 , i98i/«im 31 , 1903 


1903 


search Institute and Colleges, (Group ‘A’ and Group ‘B’ Non- 
tenure Posts) Recruitment Rules, 1966. namely : — - 

1. (1) These rules may be called the Forest Research In- 
stitute and Colleges, (Group ‘A’ and Group 'B' Non tenure 
posts) Recruitment (Amendment) Rules, 1980. 

(2) They shall be deemed to have come mio force on 
the 1st day of April, 1979. 

2. In the Forest Research Institute and Colleges, (Gioup'A’ 
and Group ‘B’ Non-tenure Posts) Recruitment Rules. 1966, 
for rule 9, the following rule shall be substituted, namely:— 

“9. Period of application of these rules in respect of certain 
posts. — -The provisions contained in these rules in 
respect of the posts mentioned at serial number 18, 
20 and 21 of the Schedule aforesaid shall remain in 
force up to and including the 25th September, 1979.” 


EXPLANATORY MEMORANDUM 

The recruitment rules for various Group ‘A’ and Group B' 
(Non-tenure) Posts in the Forest Research Institute and Col 
leges, Dehra Dun, were notified on 20th October, 1966. The 
rules for the posts of Research Officer (othe- than Engineenng 
and Statistics), Reseaich Officer (Statistical) ana Research Offi- 
cer (Engineering) were effective for a peiiod of 5 years i.e. till 
19th October, 1971. The validity of these uiles was subse- 
quently extended till 31st March, 1979. 

Since fresh recruitment rules have been issued on the 26lh 
September, 1979, it has been decided in consultation wilh 
the Union Public Service Commission to extend the validity of 
these recruitment rules further upto and including 25th Sep- 
tember, 1979. The retrospective operation of these rules will 
not prejudice the interest of any officer in the Forest Research 
Institute and Colleges, Dehra Dun.

MINISTRY OF IRRIGATION 

New Delhi, the 4th August, 1981 

GJ5.R. 782. — In exercise of the powers conferred by the 
proviso to article 30 of tJfie Constitution, the President hereby 
makes the following Rules regulating the method of recruitment 
to Group A and Group |B posts in the office of the Sardar 
Sarovar Construction Advisoiy Committee, namely : — 

1. Short title and comrtiencement. — (1) These rules may be 
called -the Sardar Sarovar Construction Advisory Committee 
(Group ‘A’ and Group ‘B’ posts) Recruitment Rules, 1981. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their pub- 
lication in the Official Gazette. 

2. Application. — These rules shall apply to the posts specified 
in Column 1 of the Schedule annexed to these rules. 

3. Number, classification and scales of pay. — The number 
of the said posts, their classification and the scales of pay 
attached thereto shall be as specified in Columns 2 to 4 of the 
said Schedule. 

4. Method of recruitment, age limit and other qualification, 
etc. — The Method of recrfiitment, age limit, qualifications and 
other matters relating to the said posts shall be as soecified in 
columns 5 to 13 of the schedule aforesaid. 

570 GI/81 — 2 


5. Disqualification. — No person, — 

(a) who has entered into or contracted a marriage with a 

person having a spouse living, or 

(b) who, having a spouse li/mg, has entered into or con- 

tracted a marriage with any person, 

shall be eligible for appointment to any of the said posts : 

Provided that the Central Government may, if satisfied that 
such marriage is permissible under the personal law applicable 
to such person and the other party to that marriage and that 
there are other grounds for so doing, exempt any person from 
the operation of this rule. 

6. Power to relax. — Where the Central Government is of 
opinion that it is necessary or expedient so to do, it may, by 
order for reasons to be recorded in writing, and in consulta- 
tion with Union Public Service Commission relax any of the 
provisions of these rules with respect to any class or category 
of persons or posts. 

7. Saving. — Nothing in these rules shall effect reservations, 
relaxation of age limit and other concession required to be 
provided for the Scheduled Castes, the Scheduled Tribes and 
Other special categories of persons in accordance with the 
Orders issued by the Central Government from time to time in 
(his regard.

MJKlSr^-OF RURAL RECONSTRUCTION 

New Delhi, the l2th -August 1981 

G.S.R. 783.— Institutional credit supf. ort >s crucial for 
the success of the Integrated Rural Develd? meDt Programme 
(IRDP). In order to review the extent of , su PPort to this 
programme from various credit institutions inft e c °atext 0 f 
the Vlth Five Year Plan and also to identify the\”P erat ' ona l 
difficulties being experienced in obtaining credit, u aas b cen 
decided to set up a high level Committee in the Mi5? istry . of 
Rural Reconstruction, which shall meet every quarter to'rov iew 



[RET II— RUR 3 (i)l 


rpcr ft T nm : R»rRr 22, i 98 i/«rm 31, 1903 


1909 


and discuss these issues. The Committee shall also undertake 
tours to States in order to gain first band knowledge of the 
field situation. The composition and the terms of reference 
of this Committee will be as under: — 

Composition 

1 . Dr. Manmohan Singh, Member Secretary 
Planning Commission. 

2. Sh. S-C- Varma, Secretary, 

Ministry of Rural Reconstruction 

3. Sh. M. Ramakrishnayya, Deputy Governor 
Reserve Bank of India- 

4. Sh- R.K. Kaul. Additional Secretary, 

Banking Division, 

Department of Economic Affairs. 

Ministry of Finance. 

5. Shri P.R. Dubhashi, Additional Secretary 
Deptt. of Agriculture and Cooperation, 

Ministry of Agriculture. 

6. Sh. G.V. Ramakrishna, Adviser, 

Rural Development, Planning Commission 

7. Sh- N.V. Krishnan, Joint Secretary, 

Plan Finance Division, 

Ministry of Finance. 

8. Sh. R. Srinivasan, Joint Secretary, 

Department of Industrial Development 
Ministry of Industry. 

9. Shri K- Ramanujam, 

Commissioner-cum-Secretary, 

Rural Reconstruction, Government of Bihar. 

10. Sh. H.B.N. Shetty, Secretary, 

Rural Development, 

Government of Tamil Nadu. 

11. Sh. Sant Dass, Managing Director, 

Agricultural Refinance & Development 
Corporation. 

12. Sh- P.G. Muralidharan, Joint Secretary, 

Ministry of Rural Reconstruction. 

Terms of Reference 

(i) Review the availability of credit for IRDP petiodically 

(ii) Examine the credit network of commercial banks, 
Regional Rural Banks and Cooperatives in the context 
of IRDP and recommend expansion, where necessary. 

(iii) Examine the operational problems coming in the way of 
obtaining credit for iRDPand recommend appro- 
priate action to meet these problems. 

(iv) Examine whether the recent guidelines on simplification 
of procedures and relaxations with respect to credit 
for the weaker sections are being implemented by bank 
branches in the field and also see whether any further 
simplifications, relaxations etc. are necessary. 

(v) Examine the progress of prepartion and implementation 
of credit plans for IRDP blocks. The Committee will 
also review the implementation of the IRDP block 
plans prepared by the ARDc and the IRDP component 
of District Credit Plan. 

(vi) Examine to what extent the credit targets set for the 
weaker sections in the Sixth Plan are being realised.

MINISTRY OF TOURISM AND CIVIL AVIATION 

New Delhi, the 4th August, 1981 

G.S.R. 784. — In exercise of the powers conferred by the 
proviso to article 309 the Constitution, the President hereby 
makes the following rules further to amend the Civil Aviation 
Department Subordinate Offices (Group ‘C" posts) Recruitment 
Rules, 1972*, namely : — < 

1. (i) These rules may be called the Civil Aviation Depart- 
ment, Subordinate Offices (Group ‘C’ posts) Recruitment 
(Amendment) Rules, 1981. 

(ii) They shall come into force on the date of their pub- 
lication in the Official Gazette. 

2. In the Schedule to the Civil Aviation Department, Sub- 
ordinate Offices (Group ‘C’ posts) Recruitment Rules. 1972. 
against serial No. 2 relating to the post of Head Clerk : — • 

(i) for the entry under column 4, the following entrv shall 

be substituted, namely : — 

“Rs. 425-1 5-500-EB-l 5-560-20-700”; 

(ii) for the entry under column 5. the following entry shall 

be substituted, namely : — 

“Non-selection”. 

NOTE 

(i) * The original Civil Aviation Department, Subordinate 

Offices (Class HI) Recruitment Rules, 1972 (including 
the recruitment rules for the post of Head Clerk) were 
notified in the Gazette of India, vide GSR No. 1198 
dated 30-9-72 (Ministry of Tourism and Civil Aviation 
notification No. 4-VE|(10)|65, dated 14-8-72). 

(ii) Certain amendments to the Civil Aviation Department, 

Subordinate offices (Class III posts) Recruitment Rules, 
1972, inter alia, to substitue “Class III” as Group ‘C’ 
were notified in the Gazette of India, vide GSR No. 
1217 dated 22-11-80 (Ministry of Tourism and Civil 
Aviation notification No. A-11014|2|76-ES(VE|SFS) 
dated 27-10-80).

G.S.R. 786. — The following draft of rules further to amend 
the Industrial Disputes (Central) Rules, 1957, which the 
Cential Government proposes to make in exercise of the 
powers conferred by sub-section (1) of section 38 of the In- 
dustrial Disputes Act, 1947 (14 of 1947), is hereby published, 
as required by the said sub-section for the information of all 
persons likely to be affected 'thereby, and notice is hereby 
given that the said diaft rules will be taken into consideration 
on or after the expiiy of a period of forty-five days fiom the 
date of publication of this notification in the Official Gazette. 

2. Any objection or suggestion which may be received from 
any person with respect to the said draft rules befoie the 
expiry of the period so specified, will be considered by the 
Central Government.

tral) (Amendment) Rules, 1981.

(Directorate of Coordination Police Computers) 

New Delhi, the 14th August, 1981 

G.S.R. 789. — In exercise of the powers conferred by the 
proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President hereby 
makes the following rules to amend the Ministry of Home 
Affairs (Directorate of Co-ordination Police Computers) 
(Group G’ Posts) Recruitment Rules, 1977, namely : — 

1. (1) These rules may be called the Ministry of Home 
Affairs (Directorate of Coordination Police Computers) (Group 
‘C’ Posts) Recruitment (Amendment) Rules, 1981. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their 
publication in the Official Gazette. 

2. In the Schedule I to the Ministry of Home Affairs 
(Directorate of Coordination Police Computers) (Group ‘C’ 
Posts) Recruitment Rules, 1977 : — 

(1) in serial No. 1, relating to the post of Sub-Inspector,— 

(a) in column 10, for the existing entry the following 

entry shall be substituted, namely 

“By promotion, failing which by transfer on Deputa- 
tion/transfer”; 

(b) in column 11, in entry (ii) for the words “By transfer 
on deputation”, the words “By transfer on deputar 
tion /transfer” shall be substituted; 

(2) in serial No 2, relating to the post of Head Cons- 
table, — 

(a) in column 10, for the existing entry, the following 

entry shall be substituted, namely 

“By promotion, failing which by transfer on deputa- 
tion/transfer”; 

(b) in column 11, in the entry (ii), for the words “By 

transfer on deputation” the words "transfer on 
deputation/transfer” shall be substituted.

MINISTRY OF FINANCE 
(Deportment of Revenue) 

New Delhi, the 29th October, 1980 

G.SJL 790. — In exercise of the powers conferred by pro- 
viso to Article 309 of the Constitution, the President hereby 
makes the following Rules namely : — 

1. Short title and commencements.— (i) These rales may be 
called the Diiectorate of inspection (Research, Statistics and 
Publication) (iDcome-tax) Sampling Offlcer/Statislician (Sy- 
stems) Recruitment Rules, 1980. 

(ii) They shall come into force on the date of their pub- 
lication in the Official Gazette. 

2. Application, — These rules shall apnly to the post specified 
in Column 1 of the Schedule annexed hereto. 

3. No. of posts, classification and scale of pay. — The number 
of posts with classification and scales of pay attached thereto 
shall be as specified in columns 2 to 4 of the said schedule. 

4. Method of recruitment, age, qualification, etc,- -The 
method of recruitment to the said posts, age limit, qualifica- 
tion and other matters connected therewith shall he as speci- 
fied in columns 5 to 13 of the schedule aforesaid. 


5, Disqualifications. — No person, 

(i) who had entered into or contracted a marriage with a 

person having a spouse living or; 

(ii) who having a spouse living has entered into or con- 

tracted a marriage with any person. 

6 hall be eligible for appointment to the post. 

Provided that the Central Government, may if satisfied that 
such a marriage is permissible under the Personal/ Law appli- 
cable to such person and the other party to the marriage and 
there are other grounds for so doing, exempt any person from 
the operation of this rule. 

6 , Powers to relax. — Where the Central Government is of 
the opinion that it is necessary or expedient to do so, it may, 
by order and for reasons to be recorded in writing and in 
consultation with the Union Public Service Commission, relax 
any of the provisions to these rules with respect to nny class or 
category of person or post, 

7, Saving. — Nothing in these rules shall effect a reservation 
and Other concessions required to be provided for candidates 
belonging to the Scheduled Castes and Scheduled Tribes and 
other special! categories of persons in accordance with the 
orders issued by the Central Government from time to time 
in this regard.

MINISTRY OF COMMERCE 
(Office of the Chief Controller of Import* & Exports) 
ORDER 

New Delhi, the 6th August, 1981 


G.SJR. 791, — M/s. Shree Bhawani Cotton Mills & Indust- 
ries Ltd., Calcutta were granted an import licence No. PICGl 
203 1512]T|CR|76|H|79/CG. I dated 30-7-80 for Rs. 8,66,250 
for the import of One No. Skoda D. G. Se t of 860KVA 
rating with spares and accessories from Czechoslovakia. The 
firm have applied for the issue of duplicate Customs Purposes 
Copy of the said import licence on the ground that the orgi- 
nal Customs Purposes Copy has been misplaced, It is further 
stated that the licence was not registered with any Customs 
authorities. 


2. In support of this contention, the applicant has filed an 
affidavit duly sworn in before the Notary Public, U.T. of 
Delhi. I am accordingly satisfied that the original Customs 
Purposes copy has been misplaced. Therefore, in exercise of 
powers conferred under sub-clause 9(cc) of the Import (Con- 
trol) Order, 1955 dated 7-12-1955 as amended, the said ori- 

f inal Customs Proposes Copy of Import Licence No. P|CG| 
031512 dated 30-7-80 issued to Mis. Shree Bhawani Cotton 
Mills and Industries Ltd., Calcutta is hereby cancelled. 

3. The duplicate Customs Purposes Copy of the said import 
licence is being issued separately, 


[No. 1459|79|5|23|CG.I] 
G. S, GREWAL, Dy. Chief Controller of 
Imports and Exports.

MINISTRY OF INDUSTRY 
(Office of the Development Commissioner) 

(Small Scale Industries) 

New. Delhi, the 6th August, 1981 

G.S R. 792. — In exercise of the powers conferred by the 
proviso to ai title 309 of the Constitution, the President 
hereby makes the following rules regulating the method of 
recruitment to the post of Investigator (Electronics) in the 
Small Industries Development Organisation under the 
Ministry of Industry namely : — 

1. Short title and Commencement. — (1) Thes e rules may 
be called the Small Industries Development Organisation In- 
vestigator (Electronics) Recruitment Rules, 1980. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their 
publication in the official gazette. 

2. Number, classification and scale of pay. — The number 
of post, its classification and the scale of pay attached thereto 
shall be as specified in columns (2) to 14) of the Schedule 
annexed to these rules. 

3. Method of recruitment, age limit and qualifications 
etc.— The method of recruitment, age limit, qualifications 


and other matters relating to the said post shall be as speci- 
fied in columns (5) to (13) of the Schedule aforesaid. 

4. Disqualifications. — No person, 

(a) who has entered into or contracted a mariiage with 
a person having a spouse living, or 

(b) who, having a spouse living, has entered into or 
contracted a marriage with any person. 

shall be eligible for appointment to the said post : 

Provided that tho Central Govemnment may, if satisfied that 
the mariiage is permissible under the personal law applicable 
to such person and the other party to the marriage and that 
there arc other grounds for so doing exempt any person 
from the operation of this rule. 

- S. Power to relax. — Where the Central Governmnent is of 
the opinion that it is necessary or expedient so to do, it may, 
by order, tor reasons to be recorded in writing, relax any 
of the provisions of these rules with respect to any category 
of persons. 

6 . Saving.— Nothing in these rules shall affect reservations, 
relaxation of age limits and other concessions required to be 
provided for the Scheduled Castes the Scheduled Tribes and 
other special categories of persons in accordance with the 
orders issued by the Central Government from time to time 
in this regard.

NATIONAL MUSEUM OF NATURAL HISTORY 
(Department of Science and Technology) 

New Delhi, the 7th August, 1981 

G.S.R. 794. — In exercise of the powers conftrred by the 
proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President hereby 
makes the following rules regulating the method of recruit- 
ment to the post of Mali in the National Museum of Nalut al 
History of the Department of Science and Technology, 
namely : — 

1. Short title and commencement :— (1) These rules may be 
called the Department of Science and Technology, National 
Museum of Natural History (Mali) Recruitment Rules, 1981, 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their publi- 
cation in the official Gazette. 

2- Number of posts, classification and scales of pay : — The 
number of the said post, its classification and the scales of 
pay attached thereto, shall be as specified in column* 2 to 4 
of the Schedule annexed to these rules. 

3. Method of recruitment, ag e limit and other qualification 
etc : — The method of recruitment, age limit, qualification and 
other matters relating to the said post shall be as specified in 
columns 5 to 13 of the said Schedule, 


[d° 1 1 ( 4 1 ) / a o-TUT Tff] 

TfoTiO 'wr-T^t, *{*■: ttfTT 

4. Disqualification : — No person, — 

(a) who has entered into or contracted a marriage with 
a person having a spouse living, or 

(b) who, having a spouse living, has entered into or 
contracted a marriage with any person, shall be 
eligible for appointment to the said post : 

Provided that the Central Government may, if satisfied 
that such marriage is permissible under the personal law appli- 
cable to such person and the other party to the marriage 
and that there are other grouuds for so doing, exempt any 
person from the operation of this rule. 

5. Power to relax : — Where the Central Government is of 
opinion that it is necessary or expedient so to do, it may, 
by order, for reasons to be recorded in writing, relax any of 
the provisions of these rules with respect to any class or cate- 
gory of persons. 

6. Saving : — Nothing in these rules shall affect reservations, 
relaxation of age limit and other concessions required to be 
provided for the Scheduled Castes, the Scheduled Tribes and 
other special categories of persons in accordance with the 
orders issued by the Central Government from time to time in 
this regard.

MINISTRY OF AGRICULTURE 
(Department of Food) 

New Delhi, the 6th August, 1981 

G.S R 796.— In exercise of the powers conferred by the proviso to article 309 of the Constitution and in supersession of the Natio- 
nal Sugar Institute, Kanpur (Group ‘C’ Posts) Rules, 1958 in so far as they relate to the post of Foreman, except as respects things done 
or omitted to be done before such supersessions, the President hereby makes the following rules regulating the method of recruitment 
to the post of Foreman in the National Sugar Institute, Kanpur, namely:— 

1 . Short title and commencement.— (1) These rules may be called the National Sugar Institute, Kanpur (Foreman) Recruitment 
Rules, 1981. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their publication in the Official Gazette. 

2. Number of post, classification and scale of pay, — The number of the said post, its classification and the scale of pay attached 
thereto shall be as specified in columns 2 to 4 of the Schedule annexed to these rules, 

3. Method of recruitment, age limit, qualifications etc.— The method of recruitment, age limit, qualifications and other matters re- 
lating to the said post, shall be as specified in columns 5 to 13 of the Schedule aforesaid 

4. Disqualification : No person, — - 

(a) who has entered into, or conti acted, a marriage with a person having a spouse living ; or 

(b) who, having a spouse living, has entered into, or contracted, a marriage with any person, 

shall be eligible for appointment to the said post : 

Provided that the Central Government may, if satisfied that such marriage is permissible under the personal law applicable to such 
person and the other party to the marriage and that there are other grounds for so doing, exempt any person from the operation of this 
rule. 



[Part IT— Sec. 3 (i)] 


1928 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : AUGUST 29, 1981/BHADRA 7, 1903 


5. Power to relax. — Where the Central Government is of opinion that it is necessary or expedient so to do, it may, by order, for 
reasons to be lecorded in writing, relax any of the provisions of these mles with respect to any class or category of persons. 

6 . Saving. — Nothing in these rules shall effect l enervations, relaxation of age limit and other concessions lequiied to be provided 
for the Scheduled Castes, the Scheduled Tribes and other special categories of persons in accordance with the orders issued by the Cen- 
tral Government from time to time in this regard.

(Department of Agriculture and Cooperation) 

New Delhi, the 7tb August, 1981 

G.S.R. 797. — In exercise of the powers conferred by the proviso to article 309 of the Constitution and in supersession of the Agricul- 
tural Prices Commission (Gestetncr Operator) Recruitment Rules, 1968, except as respects things done or omitted to be done before such 
supersession, the President hereby makes the following rules regulating the method of reciuitment to the post of Gestetner Operator in 
the Agricultural Prices Commission, Department of Agriculture and Cooperation, Ministry of Agriculture, namely: — 

1. Short title and commencement: — (1) These rules may be called the Agricultural Prices Commission (Gestetner Operator) Re- 
cruitment Rules, 1981. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their publication in the Official Gazette. 

2. Number, classification, scale of pay:— -The number of the said post, its classification and the scale of pay attached thereto shall 
be as specified in columns 2 to 4 of the Schedule annexed to these rules. 

3. Method of recruitment, age limit, qualifications, etc: — The method of recruitment, age limit, qualifications and other matters 
relating to the said post shall be as specified in columns 5 to 13 of the said Schedule. 

4. Disqualification: — No person, — 

(a) who has entered into or contracted a marriage with a person having a spouse living, or 

(b) who, having a spouse living, has entered into or contracted a marriage with any person, 
shall be eligible for appointment to the said post : 

Provided that the Central Government may, if satisfied that such marriage is permissible under the personal law applicable to such 
person and the Other party to the marriage and that there ere other grounds for so drmg, exempt any person firm <be rj-mt'tr of ifis 
rule. 



1931 


[«nill — w°* 3(i)] , 2 », i98i/»m 7, i#oa 

5, Power to relax: — Whore the Central Government is of opinion that it is necessary or expedient so to do. It may, by order, for rea- 
sons to be recorded in writing, relax any of the provisions of these rules with respect to any class or category of persons. 

6. Saving:— Nothing in these rules shall affect reservations, relaxation of age limit, and other concessions required to be provided 
for the Scheduled Castes, the Scheduled Tribes and other special categories of persons in accoidancc with the orders issued by the Central 
Government from time to time in this regard.

(Department of Agriculture) 

New Delhi, the 17th August, 1981 

G.S.R. 798. — In exercise of the powers conferred by section 
22 of the National Cooperative Development Corporation Act, 
1962 (26 of 19621, the Central Government hereby makes the 
following rules further to amend the National Cooperative 
Development Corporation Rules, 1975, namely : 

(1) These rules may be called the National Cooperative 

Development Corporation (Amendment) Rules, 1981. 

(2) They shall come into force on the dat* of their pub- 

lication in the Official Gazette. 

2. In the National Cooperative Development Corporation 
Rules, 1975, for the Form E, the following shall be substituted, 
namely : 

FORM E 
[See rule 14(b)] 

The report shall bo in tho form of a narrative and shall 
contain — 

(i) An account of the activities of the National Co- 
peratlve Development Corporation to plan, promote 
and finance programmes ft pursuance of the provi- 
sions of section 9 of the Act. 

(11) Subsidiary statements and schedules in support of 
the narrative.

MINISTRY OF FINANCE 
(Department of ReYennc) 

New Delhi, the 29th August, 1981 

G.S.R. 804. — In exercise of the powers conferred by sub- 
rule (1) of rule 8 of the Central Excise Rules, 1944, and In super- 
session of the notification of the Government of India in the 
Minislry of Finance (Department of Revenue and Insurance) 
No. J 22/71 -Central Excises, dated the 1st June, 1971, the Central 
Government hereby exempts artificial or synthetic resins 
specified in column (2) of the Table below and falling under 
Item No. 15A of the First Schedule to the Central Excises and 
Salt Act, 1944 (1 of 1944) from so much of the duty .of excise 
leviable thereon as is in excess of the rate specified in the corres- 
ponding entry in column (3) of the said Table. 

TABLE 


SI. Description Rate 

No. ■ 

0) (2) (3) 


1. Alkyd resins and maleic resins including 
fumeric resins whether or not modified (but 
excluding moulding powders or compounds in 

any form) Nil. 

2. Phenolic resins 2j)% 

ad valorem 

3. Terpene phenolic resins 20% 


ad valorem 


Explanation 

For the purposes of this notification — 

(i) the expression “alkyd resins” means synthetic resins 
manufactured by the interaction of polybasic acids and/or 
anhydrides, polyhydric alcohols and monobasic fatty acids 
and includes chemically modified alkyd resins and liquid alkyd 
resins but does not include blends or mixtures of alkyd resins 
with other artificial or synthetic resins; 

(ii) the expression “maleic resins including filmeric resins” 
means synthetic resins manufactured by reacting maleic acid 
and/or maleic anhydride and/or fumeric acid with one or more 
of the following components, namely, polyhydric alcohol, 
resins drying oils, terpenes and uns turated hydrocarbons and 
includes chemically modified maleic resins but docs not include 
blends of maleic resins with other artificial or synthetic resins; 

(iii) the expression “phenolic resins” means synthetic 
resins manufactured by reacting iny of the phenols with an 
aldehyde and includes chemically modified phenolic resins and 
liquid phenolic resins but does not include blends of the phenolic 
resins with other artificial or synthetic resins; and 

(iv) the expression “terpene phenolic resins” means synthetic 
resins manufactured by reacting any of the phenols with ter- 
penes or terpene alcohols and includes chemically modified 
terpene phenolic resins and liquid terpene phenolic resins but 
does not include blends of the terpene phenolic resins with 
other artificial or synthetics-resins. 

[Notification No.M^/Sl-C.E./F. No. 93/30/80-CX-3] 
G.N. BHAGCHANDAN1, Under Secy.
